<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The source narrator reporting Muhammad&#x27;s explicitly attributed speech or action; narrator framing remains part of the account. Abu As-Samh reports the Messenger&#x27;s request and his act of concealing him during ghusl.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Abu As-Samh said: &quot;I used to serve the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) and when he wanted to perform Ghusl he said: &#x27;Turn your back.&#x27; So I turned my back to him and concealed him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 224]</p>

## Narration 225

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A225)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 1</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 225]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/225" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 225</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i   225 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا يَعْقُوبُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنْ أَبِي مُرَّةَ، مَوْلَى عَقِيلِ بْنِ أَبِي طَالِبٍ عَنْ أُمِّ هَانِئٍ، رضى الله عنها أَنَّهَا ذَهَبَتْ إِلَى النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَوْمَ الْفَتْحِ فَوَجَدَتْهُ يَغْتَسِلُ وَفَاطِمَةُ تَسْتُرُهُ بِثَوْبٍ فَسَلَّمَتْ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قُلْتُ أُمُّ هَانِئٍ ‏.‏ فَلَمَّا فَرَغَ مِنْ غُسْلِهِ قَامَ فَصَلَّى ثَمَانِيَ رَكَعَاتٍ فِي ثَوْبٍ مُلْتَحِفًا بِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/225" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The source narrator reporting Muhammad&#x27;s explicitly attributed speech or action; narrator framing remains part of the account. Umm Hani reports the Prophet&#x27;s ghusl, conversation and eight-unit prayer.</p>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Umm Hani&#x27; that she went to the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) on the day of the Conquest (of Makkah) and found him performing Ghusl while Fatimah was concealing him with a garment. She gave him Salams and he said: &quot;Who is this?&quot; She said: &quot;Umm Hani&#x27;.&quot; When he had finished his Ghusl he stood up and prayed eight Rak&#x27;ahs wrapped in a garment</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 225]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/225" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उम्म-ए-हानी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के वो फ़तह मक्का के दिन नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास गयीं, तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) उन्हें ग़ुस्ल करते हुए मिले, फ़ातिमा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को एक कपड़े से आड़ किए हुए थीं, (उम्म-ए-हानी कहती हैं) मैंने सलाम किया, तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पूछा: ये कौन है? मैंने अर्ज़ किया: उम्म-ए-हानी हूँ, तो जब आप ग़ुस्ल से फ़ारिग़ हुए, तो खड़े हुए और एक ही कपड़े में जिसे आप लपेटे हुए थे आठ रकअतें पढ़ें।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/225" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ام ہانی رضی اللہ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ وہ فتح مکہ کے دن نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس گئیں، تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم انہیں غسل کرتے ہوئے ملے، فاطمہ رضی اللہ عنہا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو ایک کپڑے سے آڑ کیے ہوئے تھیں، ( ام ہانی کہتی ہیں ) میں نے سلام کیا ۱؎، تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پوچھا: یہ کون ہے؟ میں نے عرض کیا: ام ہانی ہوں، تو جب آپ غسل سے فارغ ہوئے، تو کھڑے ہوئے اور ایک ہی کپڑے میں جسے آپ لپیٹے ہوئے تھے آٹھ رکعتیں پڑھیں۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/225" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Umm-e-Hani (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke wo Fatah Makkah ke din Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas gayin, to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) unhe ghusl karte hue mile, Fatima (Radi Allahu Anha) aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko ek kapray se aad kiye hue thin, (Umm-e-Hani kehti hain) maine salam kiya, to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne pucha: Ye kaun hai? Maine arz kiya: Umm-e-Hani hoon, to jab aap ghusl se farigh hue, to kharay hue aur ek hi kapray mein jise aap lapetay hue thay aath rak&#x27;atein parhin.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/225" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>
